What this trace represents

This is a diagnostic replay using the frozen current actor at optimizer step 5460. It is the first Soul decision found in a seeded random order of 24 easy-pool fixtures. The action was not forced, and no winning-result filter was applied. The selected combat subsequently won and finished at 48 HP.

The original and instrumented native libraries produced identical full action lists, state hashes, trace hashes, potion records and combat outcomes on all 24 cases when inference was fixed to one state per batch. The initial multi-state batching check differed on 5 cases; that check is retained separately and is not the source of this report. Production batching can give different close decisions, so this is not claimed to reproduce a historical production trajectory.

Training continued throughout. No production simulator, weights, replay or training configuration was modified. The underlying fixture is a reconstructed human combat start, not a recording of the exact historical human action sequence.

Download files alongside this HTML: complete raw JSON, 32 simulation rows, full vector tree, readable summary.
